{"forms":[{"form":"capital accounts","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["An account in the net worth section of the balance sheet of an entity such as sole proprietorships and partnerships. In this context, the \"capital account\" (or, in cases of more than one owner, the \"sum of all individual capital accounts\") is the residual difference between total assets minus total liabilities, or the \"net worth\" of the entity. The term should not be confused with similar terms such as \"working capital,\" \"capital asset,\" \"capital expenditure,\" and \"capital lease.\""],"topics":["accounting","business","finance"]},{"glosses":["In corporations \"capital stock accounts\" are roughly equivalent to capital accounts except corporations may include other capital accounts such as \"retained earnings\" which are not individually owned by the shareholders."]},{"glosses":["An account that records all transactions between domestic and foreign residents that involve a change of ownership of an asset. suppositive used to introduce a condition that may be (or prove to be) either true or false.","A keyword that invokes conditional processing: in the event that a given condition is true, execute the given statement(s) (otherwise execute other statements)."],"topics":["computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"If she hadn't told me, I wouldn't know.","type":"example"},{"text":"If he understood how this made me feel right now, he'd be O.K. with it too.","type":"example"},{"text":"If I was King of England, I'd make you my Queen.","type":"example"},{"text":"She could have used my car if the battery hadn't been flat.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Supposing that; used with past or past perfect subjunctive to indicate a counterfactual or hypothetical condition."]},{"examples":[{"text":"I wouldn't marry you if you were the last man on earth.","type":"example"},{"text":"You're finishing your chowder if you sit there all afternoon!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Supposing that; used with past or past perfect subjunctive to indicate a counterfactual or hypothetical condition.","Even if; even in the circumstances that."],"tags":["excessive","usually"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The drain's blocked — and if the drain's blocked, the water won't flow.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Considering the fact that; given that; introducing a condition that is known to be true."]},{"examples":[{"text":"If you heat water to 100° C, it boils.","type":"example"},{"text":"If it rains, it pours.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["When; whenever; every time that."]},{"examples":[{"text":"He was a great friend, if a little stingy at the bar.","type":"example"},{"text":"She won her team's admiration, if not the award, for her performance.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Although; used to introduce a concession; may..but."]},{"examples":[{"text":"If tea is expensive, coffee is completely unaffordable.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["While; used to introduce a contrast."],"tags":["literary"]},{"glosses":["Whether; used to introduce a noun clause, an indirect question, that functions as the direct object of certain verbs."],"tags":["proscribed","sometimes"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I have leftover cake if you want some.","type":"example"},{"text":"If you want to go home, I have the car keys.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Introducing a relevance conditional; {"forms":[{"form":"notices","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"not.","tags":["alternative","abbreviation"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"He took no notice of the changes, and went on as though nothing had happened.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The act of observing; perception."],"tags":["uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"A series of notices signpost patients to the X-ray department.","type":"example"},{"text":"A notice was attached to the building reminding the residents about the five hour water shortage that was happening today.","type":"example"},{"text":"I always read the death notices in the paper.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A written or printed announcement."],"tags":["countable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The sidewalk adjacent to the damaged bridge stonework shall be closed until further notice.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A formal notification or warning."],"tags":["countable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I gave her her mandatory two weeks' notice and sacked her.","type":"example"},{"text":"I can't work here any longer.
